benchmark。Section 5 用来确认机制是否跨模型成立。Appendix D 值得快速检查，因为 NVFP4、RTN、量化范围和哪些层被量化会影响结论边界。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why weight-only post-training quantization works for pretrained LLMs. Quantizing every linear layer injects errors into the hidden states at every depth, so one would expect these errors to accumulate and corrupt next-token predictions. In practice, pretrained models tolerate aggressive 4-bit quantization far better than randomly initialized models. The central question is what pretraining changes in the way quantization errors propagate.","importance":"The value is mechanistic rather than algorithmic. The paper shows that quantization robustness cannot be explained only by small weight reconstruction error: pretrained and randomly initialized weights have similar reconstruction quality, yet their hidden-state error growth differs sharply. This connects inference-time compression to properties acquired during pretraining. The main implication is that pretraining shapes the network so that layerwise perturbations are partially self-correcting, and the remaining final-state error is filtered by the geometry of the LM head. This is relevant to quantization, low-precision training, robustness analysis, and the study of representation geometry in LLMs.","technical_approach":"The authors compare paired full-precision and quantized forward passes on the same inputs. Standard linear projections in attention and MLP blocks are quantized to NVFP4 with round-to-nearest, while the LM head and other operations remain in BF16. At each layer, the hidden-state error is decomposed into inherited error from earlier layers and newly introduced error from the current quantized block. An exact norm decomposition shows that error growth depends not only on the magnitude of the new error, but also on its inner product with the inherited error. The key empirical finding is that, in pretrained models, newly introduced block error tends to point against the inherited error, causing partial cancellation. The second part studies the final hidden-state error after it reaches the LM head. The surviving error can be sizable in hidden space, but its effect on output scores, especially for top-ranked tokens, is muted by the high-dimensional LM-head geometry.","experiments":"A concrete example is Qwen3-32B quantized directly to NVFP4 without calibration: average accuracy over six zero-shot benchmarks drops by only 0.43 percentage points. Weight reconstruction appears very close to the original weights, with cosine similarity around 0.996, but that fact alone is insufficient. Randomly initialized models have nearly the same reconstruction error, yet their hidden-state discrepancy is about 5.5 times larger. The two proposed mechanisms are tested across Qwen3 models at multiple scales, OLMo3, Gemma3, and the OLMoE mixture-of-experts model. The empirical story is consistent: pretrained models exhibit slower hidden-error growth due to layerwise cancellation, and the LM head reduces the impact of remaining errors on high-probability token predictions.","reading_guide":"A focused skim should take 20 to 30 minutes. Start with Figure 1, since it establishes the gap between pretrained and randomly initialized models under matched quantization error. Then read Section 3 carefully: the exact decomposition of hidden-error growth is the main technical contribution. Section 4 is the second important part, explaining how final hidden-state perturbations translate into score and log-probability changes through the LM head. Section 5 is useful for checking whether the mechanisms persist across dense and MoE models. Appendix D is worth a quick pass to understand the NVFP4 setup, round-to-nearest quantization, and which modules are left unquantized.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"从误差累积和抵消的角度解释了 PTQ 为什么 work，揭示了预训练赋予模型的某种内在鲁棒性结构，值得关注其分析视角。","en":"Explains why PTQ works from the perspective of error accumulation and cancellation, revealing an intrinsic robust structure endowed by pretraining; the analytical perspective is worth noting."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Tsinghua University","Bosch AI Research","Affiliation:College of AI, Tsinghua University","Affiliation:Bosch AI Research, Renningen"],"authors":["Yuxiang Chen","Michael Beyer","Jun Zhu","Jianfei Chen"],"notable_authors":["Jun Zhu","Jianfei Chen"],"tags":["Post-Training Quantization","Error Accumulation","Pretrained Models","Robustness","Mechanism Analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11716","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.11716","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.10657","title":"Quantifying the Memorization-to-Generalization Transition: Scaling Laws and Phase Structure in Grokking","abstract":"Neural networks trained past memorization frequently undergo a delayed transition to generalization, a phenomenon known as grokking. Existing work measures language specificity using the entropy of each neuron's language-wise probabilities of being active, where a neuron is considered active when its activation value is positive. distribu","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_10993.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 mLLM 中“语言特异神经元”识别标准失真的问题。已有方法通常把单个神经元在某语言上的激活简化成“是否大于 0”的二值事件，再用跨语言激活概率的熵来衡量语言特异性。这个定义有两个明显缺口：一是直接丢掉了负激活区间的信息；二是把每种语言各自独立统计，无法表达不同语言激活分布之间的相对位置、重叠程度和聚类结构。论文要解决的不是再做一次 interpretability 可视化，而是给出一个更贴近 mLLM 共享参数空间事实的神经元选择准则：把语言特异性定义为“不同语言在该神经元上的完整激活分布是否可分”，并进一步区分单语言神经元和多语言联盟神经元。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接影响不在于改进 loss 或训练 recipe，而在于提供了一个更可靠的分析工具，用来观察多语言预训练后参数空间如何分工。它的价值主要有三层。第一，方法上把语言神经元从“正激活频率高”改写成“分布结构可区分”，这会影响后续很多关于跨语言迁移、语言干预、参数编辑的分析结论。第二，它证明负激活区间里确实存在功能性语言控制神经元，这对理解 GLU/MLP 中抑制性表征很重要。第三，它把多语言共享结构显式化，能找到 Chinese–Japanese 这类多语言 coalition，而不是只盯着单语言专属单元。量级上，这更像是 interpretability 与 causal probing 方法学上的一次校正，而不是改变主流预训练范式的工作。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Distribution-aware Language Neuron, DLN。对象是带 GLU 前馈层的自回归 Transformer。对每个 MLP 神经元，作者不再统计“某语言样本中激活为正的比例”，而是收集该神经元在不同语言语料上的连续激活值，估计每种语言对应的激活分布。随后用 pairwise overlap coefficient 衡量任意两种语言在该神经元上的分布重叠程度。重叠越小，说明该神经元越能区分这两种语言；重叠矩阵进一步被用来做聚类，从而得到语言簇结构。\n\n在这个框架下，作者定义了两类神经元。SLN 是 single-language neuron，即某一种语言与其他语言分布明显分离；MLN 是 multi-language neuron，即一组语言内部相近、但与其他语言整体分离。这个定义比熵式方法更一般，因为它允许“所有语言都在正区间，但分布位置不同”的情况，也允许“目标语言主要落在负区间”的情况。\n\n论文还强调了干预位置。除了常见的 gate activation site，他们还在 gate-up product site 上验证。后者更接近 GLU 实际写回残差流前的有效贡献。已有基线在这个位置效果明显变差，而 DLN 由于建模完整分布，仍能保持选择性。因而这篇工作的关键创新不是某个复杂模型，而是把神经元选择问题从二值统计改成了连续分布比较，再把多语言关系显式编码进选择准则。","experiments":"实验主评估在 Llama-3.1-8B 和 SmolLM3-3B-Base 上进行，使用两个 held-out corpora，并在附录补充了另外三个模型的验证。评估方式不是只看相关性，而是做 causal ablation：删掉被选中的神经元，观察目标语言能力下降和非目标语言副作用。\n\n对 SLN，DLN 选出的神经元在单位神经元粒度上带来最高 4.9 倍的 on-target language damage，同时更好保留 off-target performance，说明它找到的是更干净的语言控制单元，而不是一般性重要神经元。对 MLN，DLN 能识别共享语言结构，例如 Chinese–Japanese coalition；对该簇做消融时，within-cluster damage 达到基线的 7.12 倍，同时所需神经元数减少到约 1/2.1，说明它不仅更准，也更稀疏。\n\n论文还给出两个有意思的分析。第一，负激活区间中存在英语 SLN，这些神经元用传统正激活率指标几乎不可见，但在干预时却能明显控制目标语言输出。第二，在 gate-up product site 上，activation-probability baseline 的有效性明显下降，而 DLN 仍保持较高 selectivity，支持作者关于“完整激活分布比符号统计更稳健”的论点。\n\n从结果性质看，这篇文章最有说服力的部分不是平均分提升，而是因果干预的选择性：更少神经元、更强目标破坏、更小旁路损伤。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先抓三处。第一看 Introduction 里 Figure 1 和方法动机，理解为什么“正激活概率 + 熵”会漏掉两类神经元：正区间内可分但熵高的，以及主要工作在负区间的。第二看方法部分关于 overlap coefficient 和 clustering 的定义，重点不是公式推导，而是它如何把语言关系从独立标量变成成对结构。第三看实验里的 causal ablation 主表和关于 Chinese–Japanese coalition、negative activation、gate-up product site 的分析图，这几处最能体现方法是否真的抓到功能单元。\n\n预计 30–45 分钟可以完成有效泛读。如果要继续深挖，建议顺着两个问题读：一是 overlap coefficient 对样本量、核密度估计或分箱方式是否敏感；二是 SLN/MLN 的判定阈值和聚类稳定性如何影响结论。对做多语言预训练的人，值得思考的是这些神经元结构是否随数据配比、tokenizer 粒度、脚本共享程度而系统变化。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper revisits how language-specific neurons are identified in multilingual LLMs. Prior work typically binarizes each neuron’s activation by checking whether it is positive, then measures language specificity via the entropy of language-wise activation probabilities. That definition is too coarse for multilingual models: it discards negative activations and ignores the relational structure among full activation distributions across languages. The paper addresses a more precise question: how to identify neurons whose activation distributions are genuinely language-distinct, including both single-language and multi-language specialization patterns.","importance":"Its value is methodological rather than recipe-changing. It does not alter pretraining itself, but it improves the tools used to analyze what multilingual pretraining has produced. That matters for at least three reasons. First, it corrects a common interpretability proxy: “positive-rate specificity” is not the same as distributional language selectivity. Second, it shows that functionally important language control can live in the negative activation regime, which is easy to miss in GLU-based MLP analysis. Third, it surfaces multi-language coalitions rather than only one-language-at-a-time neurons. dependency spans (distance shift). Using controlled synthetic tasks, the paper shows that explicit positional inductive biases (e.g., RoPE/ALiBi) can hurt OOD distance generalization in several regimes, while NoPE can be more robust. The joint objective is to transform the original representations such that the target concept becomes unpredictable, while maximally preserving concept-unrelated information. In this work, we revisit the optimal bounds of concept erasure to derive a novel class of erasure functions that naturally induce a deterministic, dual counterfactual mapping. Bridging the ga","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_11253.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"如何在连续表示空间中实现“概念擦除”（让目标概念 满足“对概念不可预测”（等价于类条件分布在擦除空间中不可区分/满足完美隐私条件），同时在某种效用度量下与原表示尽量接近（最大化保留信息）。作者推导最优擦除函数的结构，并证明这种最优擦除会自然诱导一个对偶的、确定性的 counterfactual 映射：给定一个样本在概念 i 下的表示，可以映射到概念 j 下的反事实表示轨迹。\n- 实现层（MUtE）：为避免一般非线性擦除的计算瓶颈（对抗训练、密度估计、贝叶斯优化等），提出带“平移偏置”（translational bias）的实现：假设概念在现代编码器表征中常表现为近似线性方向/平移结构，于是反事实轨迹可用近似的平移来参数化，从而同时得到擦除与反事实生成，计算上更轻。\n与 LEACE/INLP 这类线性投影相比，MUtE Specifically, how does the model distinguish between 'where to find knowledge (routing)' and 'what knowledge is found (content)' within its hidden states?","importance":"From the perspective of Mechanistic Interpretability, this paper provides a deep dive into the dynamic process of internal knowledge retrieval in LLMs. By decoupling the function of hidden states into 'content' and 'routing' and revealing their evolution across layers, it helps understand the root causes of hallucinations and provides a theoretical basis for future model intervention and knowledge editing.","technical_approach":"1. Proposes to functionally separate hidden states into 'Content' (knowledge directly supporting the answer) and 'Routing' (information controlling where subsequent layers access knowledge).\n2. Further subdivides routing into 'Parameter Routing' (pointing to knowledge in model weights) and 'Hidden-state Routing' (pointing to knowledge already extracted into hidden states).\n3. Causally separates and measures the effects of these components through carefully designed layer-wise intervention experiments on models like Qwen, Llama, and Gemma (e.g., changing the country name in a prompt and observing how hidden state changes affect the output continent).","experiments":"Experiments reveal a clear temporal pattern: in early layers, parameter routing becomes active, guiding the extraction of knowledge from weights; as knowledge is extracted and integrated into hidden states to form 'content', hidden-state routing takes effect; in later layers, a 'Routing-Content Handoff' occurs, where the model's reliance on routing information decreases, and it instead relies on the formed 'content' to generate the final answer.","reading_guide":"Read the introduction for the definitions of 'content' and 'routing', and the experimental design section to understand how interventions on hidden states separate these functions. and","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_11780.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CR","cs.NE"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究一个很具体的问题：能否只看训练后模型的权重谱结构，在不访问训练数据、也不训练 shadow model 的前提下，快速预估模型的 membership inference attack（MIA）泄露风险。作者关心的不是提出更强攻击，而是寻找一种便宜、可扩展、适合实际审计流程的隐私风险代理信号。全文的关键点在于，他们把 WeightWatcher/HT-SR 这一套原本更多用于分析泛化与训练状态的谱指标，拿来和 MIA 成功率做系统对照，并检验这些谱指标是否比传统 generalization gap 更能解释隐私泄露。","importance":"对基座预训练本身，这不是直接改进训练 recipe 的论文，但它提供了一个有启发的方向：模型是否泄露训练样本，未必只能靠昂贵的攻击评测来判断，权重层面的结构信号可能已经编码了部分风险信息。这个思路对大模型预训练、持续训练和发布前审计都有现实意义，尤其是在需要批量筛查多个 checkpoint、多个数据配比或多个训练阶段时。贡献量级偏“诊断工具与风险代理”而不是“机制定论”：它没有建立严格因果关系，也没有在 LLM 上验证，但证明了 stable rank、log α-norm 这类谱指标与 MIA 风险存在稳定相关性，而且和 generalization gap 互补，这一点对后续做大模型隐私监控、训练动态分析、checkpoint selection 都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法上很朴素，但设计是认真的。作者先用 WeightWatcher 从已训练模型的各层权重矩阵中提取谱统计量，核心考察 stable rank、log α-norm，以及若干常见谱/范数指标。其理论背景来自 heavy-tailed self-regularization：训练良好的网络，其经验谱密度往往偏离随机矩阵，呈现更强的相关结构；不同谱相位被解释为从随机、到出现 spikes、到 heavy-tailed、再到 rank collapse 的连续过程。作者的问题是，这些结构究竟更接近“泛化良好”还是“记忆过强”，以及它们与隐私泄露的关系是什么。\n\n实验框架里，他们把每个训练好的目标模型同时映射到两类量：一类是传统 generalization gap，另一类是 WW 谱指标；然后用 LiRA 等 MIA 去测真实泄露强度，再分析相关性与预测能力。论文特别区分了整体 MIA 成功率与低 FPR 区域的攻击表现，因为实际隐私审计更关心低误报条件下能否识别成员。结果显示，stable rank 与整体 MIA 成功率呈较强正相关，而 log α-norm 与低 FPR 区域的 MIA 表现呈稳定负相关。作者进一步做组合建模，发现谱指标和 generalization gap 捕捉到的不是同一件事，联合后对高风险模型的识别更好。\n\n值得注意的是，这里的“谱指标能预测隐私”并不等于“谱指标解释了隐私机制”。从全文看，作者的论证更接近经验代理：stable rank 可能反映了层有效维度和权重能量分布，log α-norm 则把尾部形状与谱尺度合在一起，因而比单纯 test-train gap 更接近模型内部表征状态。但论文没有给出严格的层级因果分析，也没有把不同层、不同训练阶段的谱演化和样本级记忆直接连起来。","experiments":"实验主要覆盖图像分类和表格分类模型，而不是语言模型，这决定了它的外推边界。作者在多个数据集和模型设置上，比较了 WeightWatcher 指标、generalization gap 与 LiRA/RMIA 风险之间的关系。核心结果有三点。\n\n第一，stable rank 对整体 MIA success 的相关性普遍强于 generalization gap。也就是说，如果目标是粗筛“哪些模型更容易被成员推断攻击打中”，stable rank 是更有用的单一信号。\n\n第二，在低 false-positive-rate 区域，log α-norm 的表现更稳定，而 generalization gap 在这一 regime 下预测力弱且不一致。这个结果很关键，因为真实审计通常不能接受高误报，低 FPR 下的攻击能力才更接近部署场景。\n\n第三，把谱指标和 generalization gap 结合起来做二分类，可以更好地区分高风险与低风险模型，说明两类信号是互补的。论文还分析了不同谱指标之间的差异，指出不是所有 WeightWatcher 指标都同样有效，stable rank 和 log α-norm 是最稳的两个。\n\n从摘要和正文可见，作者强调“cheap-to-compute”和“data-free”两点：相比 LiRA/RMIA 需要训练 shadow/reference models，这套方法只需要访问训练后权重，计算成本低很多。论文的局限也写得比较清楚：任务类型较窄，模型规模较小，没有覆盖 Transformer/LLM，也没有证明这些指标在分布外设置、不同优化器或大规模预训练中仍然稳定。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Background，抓住作者到底想替代什么：不是替代所有隐私评测，而是替代昂贵的 shadow-model 审计流程。然后重点看方法部分里对 WeightWatcher 指标的定义，尤其是 stable
